\caption{\textbf{Full-horizon forecasts can remain visibly wrong.} A fixed Goop example (source 12, seed 0) selected by median initial particle count, without inspecting errors. All 1,083 particles use the same bounds at forecasts 1 and 395. The target settles along the floor; predicted groups remain suspended or leave the box. Training and policy both differ across prediction columns, so this is an illustration of physical mismatch, not a paired training-effect estimate. Crosses show any strict box crossing, clamped to the boundary; they do not encode excursion magnitude.}
\label{fig:qualitative-goop2d}
\end{figure*}
